query.js — Multi-hop graph exploration

Usage:
  query.js --graph=<path> --source=<path> --id=<nodeId> [--hops=<N>] [--dirs-tree=<path>]

Options:
  --graph=<path>       Path to the graph file (graph.schema.json compliant)
  --source=<path>      Path to the source file to explore
  --id=<nodeId>        Start node ID(s) for exploration (comma-separated for multiple)
  --hops=<N>           Maximum hops (default: 1, must be >= 1)
  --dirs-tree=<path>   Path to Dirs-Tree.json (optional, shows implementation file paths)
  --help, -h           Display this help

Exit codes:
  0  Normal completion
  1  Error exit (invalid arguments, file not found, etc.)

Examples:
  query.js --graph=RFC-GRAPH.json --source=RFC.md --id=N0001 --hops=2
  query.js --graph=RFC-GRAPH.json --source=RFC.md --id=N0001,N0003 --hops=1
  query.js --graph=RFC-GRAPH.json --source=RFC.md --id=N0001 --dirs-tree=RFC-Dirs-Tree.json
